===Chinese===
这一日暑热如此酷烈，方才在山坡上那场徒劳的攀爬又耗去我们不少气力，要在天黑前走完去天然码头的全程，自觉难以胜任；何况日头的位置明明白白告诉我们，午后已久，要保全白昼的光阴，就得马不停蹄地赶路。

===Original===
So we decided to take it easily, and select a camp for the night close to water, on the weather slopes of the mountains. We should have liked to remain where we were, by the river, in the midst of the old Portuguese settlement, but, knowing the difficulties of the homeward journey, we felt that it would be advisable to proceed some way further on our road before camping, and so leave a shorter distance to travel on the morrow.

===Chinese===
于是我们决意从容行事，在山内向风的山坡上、傍水之处选一处过夜的营地。我们本想留在原地，留在河边这片葡萄牙人旧屯居地的当中；但归途的艰难心中有数，觉得还是先赶出一程再扎营为妥，好给明天省下短些的路程。

===Original===
We accordingly left the riverside and struck across the downs to the foot of the ravine by which we had descended on the previous day. On our way we gathered a quantity of beans for our supper.

===Chinese===
于是我们离开河边，斜穿过草冈，朝前一天下来的那道谷的谷脚走去。一路采了不少豆子，充作晚饭。

===Original===
We soon found the ravine, and began to ascend it. The foul white birds again attacked us as we climbed from rock to rock, and the ugly crabs waved their pincers at us with menacing gestures. Then we came to the lowest point on the hillside where water is found. This was at a much greater distance from the beach than it had seemed to be while we were descending on the day before; for the stream disappears in the soil at a spot at least 600 feet above the level of the sea, and to attain it from below involves a pretty stiff climb.

===Chinese===
不多时我们便寻到了那道谷，开始向上攀。我们在岩块间攀上攀下，那些肮脏的白鸟又来攻击我们，丑陋的螃蟹朝我们挥舞螯钳，做出恫吓的姿态。随后我们到了山坡上见水的最低一处。这里离滩头的距离，远比前一日下行时看去的长远；因为溪水渗没入土之处，至少高出海面 600 英尺，从下面攀到那里，得费一番不轻的脚力。

===Original===
We went still higher up the ravine, until we were close to the place where the stream issues from the ground, a short distance below the foot of the great landslip of black rocks. Here we found an admirable site for our camp. This gully, as I have explained, falls towards the shore at a very steep angle, the rocks, as it were, forming a gigantic flight of steps. We were now on one of these steps, a flat surface, about ten feet across, covered with red sand. The stream fell on to this from the step above, forming a little cascade some twelve feet in height, and, after crossing one side of the flat, fell over another wall of rock on to the step below.

===Chinese===
我们再往谷的高处走，直到紧挨溪水涌出地面之处——在那大片黑岩滑坡的坡脚以下不远。就在这里，我们寻得了一处绝佳的营址。如前所述，这道沟以极陡的角度泻向岸边，岩石层层，俨然砌成一道巨大的阶梯。我们此刻正站在其中一级台阶上——一块平坦的台面，宽约十英尺，覆着红沙。溪水从上一级台阶跌落到这里，形成一道约莫十二英尺高的小瀑布，横过台面的一侧，又翻过另一道石壁，跌到再下一级台阶上。

===Original===
The scene around us was strangely picturesque. Our step was simply a small ledge in this wilderness of broken black rocks; above us and below us were precipices and landslips. It was an excellent situation for an eagle's nest, but not an over-secure spot for a camp of men. Our narrow bed would not do for a restless sleeper: to slip off the edge of it would insure a broken neck. A coarse grass grew here and there between the rocks by the waterside, but there was no other vegetation on the bleak crags, though of course the mysterious dead trees, as everywhere else on this island, were lying thickly all around. The foul birds and the land-crabs were the sole inhabitants of this solitude.

===Chinese===
四下的景象出奇地如画。我们立足的这级台阶，不过是这片黑岩荒陬里的一方小小石台；头顶脚下，皆是绝壁与滑坡。这里安一个鹰巢再理想不过，作人的营地却谈不上多么稳当。我们这张窄床可容不得睡觉不老实的人：从床沿一滑滚落，管保摔断脖颈。水边岩缝间东一丛西一丛生着粗硬的野草，此外荒寒的危岩上再无草木；当然，那神秘的枯树——这岛上处处如此——在这里也四下密密地横陈着。这片荒寂之境，唯有肮脏的白鸟与陆蟹栖居。

===Original===
We now proceeded to make ourselves at home for the night. I collected the branches and trunks of the dead trees and built up a goodly pile of firewood, while the doctor prepared our supper. We had no saucepan with us, so the pannikin had to do duty for one. In this the doctor concocted a stew, the ingredients of which were *ch'arki*, biscuit, figs, and Trinidad beans. It turned out to be a far more tasty dish than one would have supposed.

===Chinese===
接着我们便动手安顿过夜。我去拾掇枯树的枝干，堆起一大垛柴禾，医生则备办晚饭。我们没带炖锅，小锡杯只好权充其职。医生就用它炖了一锅杂烩，原料是恰尔基风干牛肉、硬面饼、无花果和特林达迪的豆子。炖出来竟比人料想的鲜美得多。

===Original===
After dinner the saucepan was cleaned out and grog was served out in it—the last of our supply of rum. We had just lit our pipes and were settling ourselves down to a comfortable half-hour's smoke and chat before turning in (to whom is a pipe so sweet as to one camping out under the stars after the day's work?) when suddenly the doctor cried out, "Hullo, look at our beds!" I looked, and lo! to my dismay, those luxurious couches were under water.

===Chinese===
晚饭之后，锅刷净了，又用它分发了掺水的朗姆酒——那是我们最后一点朗姆酒存项。我们刚点上烟斗，正安顿下来，打算睡前舒舒服服地抽着烟聊上半个小时（一天的活计做完，在星光下露宿，一斗烟还有比这更甜的吗？），医生忽然喊道：「喂，快看咱们的床！」我一看，哎呀！那些奢华的卧榻竟泡在水里，直叫我心里发慌。

===Original===
I must explain that we had pulled up a quantity of grass and strewed it over the sand, so as to make a snug soft sleeping-place for the night. While we were enjoying our dinner, the river, unobserved by us, had risen considerably, and was now flowing over that portion of the step whereon we had made up our beds. There had been no rain to account for this, so I suppose that the sun, blazing down on the rocks, causes a great evaporation of water during the day, and that, consequently, the volume of the stream is greater after sunset.

===Chinese===
这里须说明一下：我们拔了许多青草，铺在沙上，好给夜里垫出一张安稳松软的铺位。我们享用晚饭的当口，河水悄悄涨起了许多，无人察觉，此刻正漫过台阶上我们铺床的那一片。天上没有落过一滴雨，无从解释此事；于是我想，大约是烈日炙晒岩石，白天促成大量水汽蒸腾，因而日落之后，溪流的水量反倒更大。

===Original===
So we had now to put aside our pipes and grog for a few moments and undertake some necessary engineering operations: we cleared away a channel through the natural dam of grass, stones, and sand at the lower edge of the step, and so gave a free passage to the swollen stream. The flood subsided at once, and our beds were above water again. The doctor, then, acting in his medical capacity, suggested that damp mattresses were unhealthy; so we threw a few handfuls of grass on the top of the sodden mass, and our beds were what we were pleased to call dry again.

===Chinese===
于是我们只好暂且搁下烟斗和酒，操办几项必要的工程：在台阶下缘那道由青草、石头和沙子构成的天然堤坝里清出一条水路，让暴涨的溪流畅行无阻。洪水立时退去，我们的床重又露出水面。接着，医生以行医的身份进言：潮湿的床垫有碍健康。我们便往浸透的草铺上又撒了几把干草，我们的床便又成了我们乐于称作「干」的东西。

===Original===
We lit a fire of the dead wood and kept it alight all night, so that we could occasionally warm ourselves by it; for a wind had sprung up at sunset, sweeping up the ravine from the sea, making us feel uncomfortably chilly, thinly clad as we were and having no blankets to cover us.

===Chinese===
我们用枯木生起一堆火，整夜不让它熄灭，好不时凑过去暖一暖身子；原来日落时起了风，顺着峡谷从海上刮上来，我们衣衫单薄，又没有毯子裹身，只觉寒气砭人，甚不好受。

===Original===
We soon found that it would be impossible for us both to sleep at the same time, for the land-crabs had smelt us out and swarmed down upon us from all sides. We kept watch and watch; while one slept the other tended the fire and killed the land-crabs, as they approached, with sticks and stones. The other crabs, as usual, fed on the dead. I have, in *The Cruise of the Falcon*, described the peculiarly uncanny way in which a land-crab eats his food. I saw this night, as I kept watch, at least twenty of them at a time devouring the carcasses of their slain friends. Each stood quite still, looking me straight in the face with his fixed outstarting eyes, and with an expression absolutely diabolical. He pulled the food to pieces with his two front claws, and then, with deliberate motion, brought the fragments of flesh to his mouth with one claw, and chewed them up with a slow automatic action, but still those horrible eyes never moved, but stared steadily into mine.

===Chinese===
不多时我们便发觉，两人绝不能同时入睡，因为陆蟹已嗅出我们的气味，从四面八方蜂拥而至。我们只好轮班守夜；一人睡下，另一人便照看火堆，用木棍和石块把近前的陆蟹一一击毙。其余的蟹照例大啖死去的同类。《猎鹰号巡航记》里，我描写过陆蟹进食时那副特有的阴森样子。这天夜里守夜时，我看见它们一次总有二十来只，在吞吃被杀的同伙的尸身。每一只都一动不动地站着，一双暴凸的僵眼直勾勾望着我的脸，神情十足一副魔鬼相。它用两只前螯把食物撕开，再不慌不忙地用一只螯把碎肉送到嘴边，以一种缓慢而机械的动作咀嚼起来；可那双可怕的眼睛始终一动不动，直盯盯地望进我的眼睛里。

===Original===
As we had no means of judging the time, it was difficult to divide the night into watches of even length, so we had to portion it out between us the best way we could.

===Chinese===
我们无从判断时刻，要把一夜分成长短齐整的班次颇不容易，只好尽我们所能，把夜大致均摊在两人之间。

## XIII / 十三

===Original===
A Narrow Escape

===Chinese===
死里逃生

===Original===
We started early on the following morning, November 23, and reached the summit of the landslip before the sun had heated the black rocks, and the layer of close air immediately over them, to that high temperature which we had found so insupportable on the previous day.

===Chinese===
翌日清晨，11 月 23 日，我们早早启程，趁太阳还没有把黑岩烤热、把紧贴岩面的那层滞闷空气晒到头一天那令人难以忍受的高温，便已登上了滑坡的顶端。

===Original===
We managed to ascend the cliff which hangs over the landslip without accident, but it was anxious work, and we experienced a sense of relief when we found ourselves safe once more on the upper plateau.

===Chinese===
我们总算平安无事地登上了悬在滑坡上方的那面峭壁，只是这活计干得人提心吊胆；待重新安然立在高处的高台上，才觉出如释重负。

===Original===
From here we took a shortcut across the groves of tree-ferns towards the head of the cascade ravine, and came unexpectedly upon a green valley in the middle of the plateau which we had not seen before, and which is, without doubt, the most beautiful place on the island. At the bottom of it a cool stream flowed through thickly-growing ferns and grass. The scenery all round us was of a soft and pleasing character, very strange to us after the dreary barrenness of the mountain slopes beneath this elevated and almost inaccessible garden.

===Chinese===
从这里我们抄了近路，穿过一丛丛树蕨林，朝瀑布谷的谷首走去，不料在高台中部与一道翠绿的山谷不期而遇——我们先前竟从未见过它，而它毋庸置疑是全岛最美的地方。谷底一道清凉的溪水，从繁密的蕨类和草丛间流过。四围景致温婉悦目；我们刚从这座高悬于上、几乎人迹难至的花园下方的荒凉枯瘠山坡上来，此刻只觉恍如别一世界。

===Original===
We might have been in some fair vale of Paraguay, instead of on the summit of rugged Trinidad. Here were gently sloping green hills that shut out all view of the jagged peaks. The vegetation was of a more luxuriant nature than in any other portion of the island; tall grasses, bushes, and plants of various kinds, most of them covered with flowers, carpeted the soft red soil, while the tall and beautiful tree-ferns stood in scattered clumps, casting a pleasant shade with their fronds of darker green. Even the dead trees were not so melancholy in appearance as elsewhere on the island; for from their branches—as well as from those of the older bushes and tree-ferns—there hung swaying festoons of a parasitic plant something like the Spanish moss that covers the pines and live-oaks of Florida, but more beautiful, for this was of a silvery white colour.

===Chinese===
我们恍若置身巴拉圭某处明媚的山谷，而不是站在蛮荒嶙峋的特林达迪之巅。这里尽是绿冈缓坡，把参差奇峰的景致遮了个严实。草木之繁茂，胜过岛上任何去处：高草、灌木与形形色色的花草，多半缀着花朵，把松软的红土铺成一片锦绣；高挑秀美的树蕨三五成丛，以色泽较深的蕨叶投下宜人的荫凉。连枯树也不似岛上别处那般面目愁惨：枝桠上——老灌木和老树蕨的枝桠上也一样——垂挂着串串摇曳的花彩，是一种寄生植物，模样有几分像覆满佛罗里达松树与活栎树的西班牙苔藓（Spanish moss），却更为美丽，因为它通体银白。

===Original===
Besides those tyrants of Trinidad, the birds and land-crabs, mice, flies, ants, earwigs, and big spiders dwelt in this happy valley.

===Chinese===
除了特林达迪的两霸——禽鸟与陆蟹——之外，老鼠、苍蝇、蚂蚁、蠼螋和大蜘蛛也栖居在这座快活谷里。

===Original===
From here we walked to the head of our ravine, where the principal grove of tree-ferns crowns the cliffs, and now we looked down upon the *Alerte*, seeming very small from this dizzy height, “and yon tall anchoring bark, diminished to her cock; her cock, a buoy almost too small for sight.” We observed that the wind was blowing rather freshly from an unusual quarter—northwest—making this a lee shore to our vessel, but there were no signs of bad weather in the sky.

===Chinese===
从这里我们走到我们那道谷的谷首，最茂盛的一片树蕨林正冠盖在崖顶。此刻我们俯瞰「阿勒特号」，在这令人目眩的高处她显得那样渺小，「那边高高的泊船，缩成了一叶小艇；那小艇，又成了一枚浮标，几乎小得目不能见。」我们还注意到，风正从不常刮的方向——西北——相当劲急地吹来，使这里成了我们船只的下风岸；好在天空里并无坏天气的迹象。

===Original===
While descending the ravine we were shut in by the walls of rock, so that we were unable to see the yacht; but on reaching a point just above the cascade we again commanded a view over the whole roadstead, and lo! we found, to our dismay, that the *Alerte* was no longer lying at her anchorage, nor was she anywhere in sight.

===Chinese===
下谷途中，岩壁夹峙，我们望不见游艇；但一到瀑布上方不远处，整片锚地又尽收眼底——哎呀！我们愕然发现，「阿勒特号」已不在泊位之上，四下里也全不见她的踪影。

===Original===
We stood and stared round the horizon, scarcely believing the evidence of our eyes. Not an hour before we had looked down upon her from the mountain, riding snugly to her anchor, with sails stowed. What possible mischance could have occurred since then?

===Chinese===
我们伫立着环望水平线，几乎不敢相信自己的眼睛。不到一个钟头之前，我们还从山上俯瞰过她：帆已收妥，安安稳稳地伏在锚上。打那以后，究竟可能出了什么变故？

===Original===
We proceeded to the pier, on to which we perceived that the sea was breaking much more heavily than when we had landed on it, and from here we were enabled to see further round the coast to the northwest. Then we caught a glimpse of our vessel just before she rounded, and was hidden by, the first promontory. She was about two miles away, with all plain sail set, beating against the wind towards the northern end of the island.

===Chinese===
我们走向天然码头，看得出浪头扑上码头的势头，比我们登岸那天凶猛得多了；从这里，我们的视野得以沿海岸朝西北伸展开去。恰在她绕过第一道海岬、被岬角遮没之前，我们瞥见了我们的船。她在约两英里外，满帆尽张，正顶风抢行，朝岛的北端驶去。

===Original===
We surmised that those on board had become anxious about our safety, and were sailing round the island in order, if possible, to discover where we were—a course which they had no right to undertake, seeing that the doctor and myself had not yet been two and a half days away, and were not likely to have lost ourselves. Besides which, I knew that there was no one on board competent to take charge of the vessel on a cruise of this sort. Under these circumstances I was in anything but an amiable temper, more especially as the doctor and myself were now fagged out by our exertions, and had been looking forward to a square meal, and some good red wine with it, on our return on board.

===Chinese===
我们猜想船上的人惦记我们的安危，便驾船环岛巡驶，想探明我们的下落——这一举动他们本无权擅自做主：要知道，我和医生离船还不满两天半，断不至迷了路。何况我清楚，船上并没有一个能胜任指挥这等巡航的人。处此情形，我的心绪实在说不上和善，尤其我和医生已劳累得筋疲力尽，一路只盼着回到船上饱餐一顿，再配上几杯上好的红葡萄酒。

===Original===
As it appeared that they were bent on sailing round the island, and might not be off the pier again until the following day—for the yacht was evidently progressing very slowly, plunging her nose constantly into the steep head seas—I determined to recall them, if possible. So we hurried back to a slope near the cascade where the grass was growing thickly, and applied a match to it. As I expected, there was soon a great blaze, and a dense volume of smoke arose which must have made itself visible for many miles around. The wind fanned the flames, and the fire crept slowly up the mountainside wherever the dry grass afforded a track for it; the dead trees, too, began to burn fiercely, and we discovered that we had started a somewhat larger conflagration than we had intended, and had set the whole of this side of the island on fire.

===Chinese===
看这架势，他们是一意要环岛航行了，说不定要到第二天才会再驶回天然码头外——因为游艇分明行得极慢，船首不住扎进陡峭的迎头浪里——我便打定主意，倘若可能，要把他们召回来。于是我们匆匆赶回瀑布附近一片草木茂密的坡地，划了根火柴点上去。果如所料，顷刻间便腾起大火，一股浓烟冲天而起，方圆许多英里之外必然望得见。风煽着火势，火沿着山坡缓缓爬升，凡干草铺得出路的地方都烧将过去；枯树也开始熊熊燃烧。我们这才发现，自己放的这场火远比本意闹得大，竟把岛的整整这一面都点着了。

===Original===
However, it produced the desired effect: we saw the yacht sail clear of the point again, on the starboard tack, bear away, and run down the coast towards us. And now, at the suggestion, as I afterwards learnt, of Arthur Cotton, who ought to have known better, but who, as having been here before with me, professed to be well acquainted with the pilotage of Trinidad, the anchor was let go, to my horror, quite close to the edge of the breakers. Our vessel was now in very convenient proximity to the end of the pier, it is true, but in a most perilous position: for no sea-room had been allowed her—a very necessary precaution under these cliffs, where the wind is never steady—and I saw that, when the anchor was weighed again, we should run great risk of being carried on to the rocks by the rollers before we could get the yacht under command.

===Chinese===
不过，火到底收到了预期的效果：我们望见游艇重新驶出海岬，抢着右舷风，转舵顺浪，沿海岸朝我们驰来。这时——我后来才得知——是亚瑟·科顿（Arthur Cotton）出的主意把锚抛了下去；此人本该更明白些轻重，只因先前随我来过此地，便自诩深谙特林达迪的引航门道。锚落之处，竟紧贴着碎浪的边缘，吓得我魂飞魄散。不错，我们的船此刻离天然码头尽头确实近便得很，处境却凶险至极：没有给她留出半点回旋的水域——在这些崖壁之下，风从无定准，这一防范本是万不可省的——我看得分明，待到再度起锚，不等游艇受舵控制，我们便大有被滚浪掼上礁石之虞。

===Original===
It may be imagined what was my condition of mind when I realised all this, and the doctor was naturally as savage as myself. We stood on the pier and watched the men as they lowered the sails and then launched the whaleboat in order to fetch us off. Powell, Pursell, and two of the paid hands manned the boat. The sea was now so high that they could not approach very near to the shore. The waves were dashing high up the sides of the pier, and, in recoiling, rushed across the end of it in the form of a cascade.

===Chinese===
可以想见，等我明白了这一切，心里是何等光景；医生自然也同我一样怒不可遏。我们站在天然码头上，看着船员们落下帆，随即放下捕鲸艇来接我们。鲍威尔（Powell）、珀塞尔（Pursell）和两名受雇水手上了艇。此刻浪头极高，他们无法靠岸太近。波浪高高地扑打着码头的侧面，退落时又化作一道瀑布，漫过码头尽头奔泻而去。

===Original===
Seeing that we must swim for it, we took off our coats and placed them in a hole at the top of the rocks. I shouted to those in the boat to keep some distance off, and throw a life-buoy with a line attached to it towards the pier, so that we could jump in and be hauled off by it. This was done. Choosing my time I leapt in, held on to the line, the boat was pulled seaward out of reach of the breakers and I clambered on board. Then we returned for the doctor. He stood on the pier, waiting for his opportunity, but one much higher roller than the rest came up and swept him off into the sea. Luckily, he was not dashed against any of the rocks, but managed to swim out clear of the recoil, while we backed towards him and took him on board.

===Chinese===
眼见非泅水不可，我们脱下上衣，塞进岩顶的一个洞穴里。我朝艇上的人大喊，叫他们保持些距离，把一只系着绳索的救生圈朝码头抛来，我们便可跳进水里，由它拽出去。他们依言照办。我瞅准时机纵身入水，攥牢绳索，小艇被拉向外海、避开了碎浪，我攀上艇去。随后我们折返去接医生。他立在码头上静候时机，不料一个比先前都高出许多的滚浪卷上来，把他扫进了海里。所幸他没有撞上任何礁石，挣扎着游离了回头浪的势力，我们倒划着艇迎上去，把他拉了上来。

===Original===
Once safe on the deck of the *Alerte* I listened to an explanation of the extraordinary manoeuvres which had been taking place.

===Chinese===
一登上「阿勒特号」的甲板，安然无恙，我便听人解释起方才那一连串离奇调度的缘由。

===Original===
It seemed that either the yacht had dragged her anchor, or it was supposed that she had dragged her anchor—for the opinions on the matter were at variance—so the anchor was weighed, and, of course, as the chain got short, the yacht, even if she had not done so before, began to drag at a merry pace. Then sail was hoisted. By this time she had drifted very close to the rocks, but, as far as I understand, she was filling and would soon have been in safety again, when, for some reason or other, down went the anchor, and she lay rolling about close under the rocky Ness and the dangerous islets that lie off it. Up came the anchor once more, and this time the yacht drove so very near to the rocks that everyone on board gave her up as lost, and some were looking out for the safest spot on shore to swim to. A high sea was breaking over the cliffs—one touch and she would have broken up. And now, as by a miracle—for I don’t know how it happened, and no one on board seems to have known—the vessel got way on her and forged ahead, so that she became manageable, and was steered out to sea, clear of danger.

===Chinese===
原来，要么是游艇走了锚，要么是众人以为她走了锚——这桩公案众说纷纭——于是起了锚；自然，锚链一短，游艇便开始撒欢似的拖着锚疾漂，哪怕先前并未如此。接着又升起了帆。这时她已漂得离礁石极近；但据我理解，当时她帆已吃满风，眼看就要脱险归安，却不知出于什么缘故，锚又抛了下去，她便紧挨着嶙峋的尼斯岬（Ness）和岬外那几座凶险的小岛，横冲直撞地翻腾摇摆。锚再一度起了上来，这一回游艇直往礁石上逼，近得船上人人只道她完了，有些人已在物色岸上最稳妥、可以泅水逃命的去处。巨浪正扑过崖顶——只消碰上一下，她便粉身碎骨。就在这时，仿佛有神迹一般——因为我不知道这是怎么成的，船上似乎也没人知道——船身得了进速，硬是向前拱去，于是重新受了控，被驶出外海，脱离了险境。

===Original===
That she had been very nearly wrecked there can be no doubt, and that this had been due to very awkward handling was also certain. I was myself much to blame for the serious risk the poor old vessel had incurred. Had I left the doctor in charge on board, in his capacity of mate, while I was exploring the island, he would, no doubt, have extricated the yacht from her difficulty as soon as she began to drag—an easy task. I did not consider that there was anyone else among the volunteers capable of undertaking the responsibility of command, but I was under the impression—wrongly it seems—that the five paid hands on board would have had the common sense to give her more chain when they perceived that the wind was freshening. Ted, for instance, was bos’n, and might have taken it upon himself to do this, as was indeed his understood duty when no officers were on board.

===Chinese===
她险些失事，这是毋庸置疑的；而这场大险全系操作笨拙所致，也是确定无疑。可怜的老船遭此大祸，我自己难辞其咎。我上岛探察时，若让医生以大副的身份留守船上，那么她一开始走锚，他无疑便能即刻解围——那本是轻而易举之事。我原认为志愿队员里没有第二人担当得起指挥的重任，却又以为——如今看来是错了——船上那五名受雇水手总该有这点常识：一见风势转劲，便给她多放些锚链。譬如特德，他就是水手长，本可当机立断亲自去放，船上没有军官时，这本是他心照不宣的职责。

===Original===
For the first and only time during the cruise these men lost their heads, and, having no recognised leader to direct them, each volunteered his own opinion as to what should be done, or as to whether the vessel was dragging at all; but, as far as I can make out, with one man giving one order at one end of the vessel, and another man giving a contradictory order at the other end, nothing at all was done until it was almost too late.

===Chinese===
整个巡航期间，这些人只此一遭乱了方寸——既没有公认的领头人指挥，人人便争着献策，该做什么也好，船到底走没走锚也好，各执一词；但据我拼凑出的情形：船头一人发一道令，船尾另一人又发一道相反的令，结果什么也没办成，直到几乎来不及。

===Original===
I made up my mind never from this time to leave the vessel, even for a short time, without putting someone definitely in charge, even if he were an incompetent person.

===Chinese===
从此我打定主意：日后哪怕离开船只片刻，也必先指定一人明确掌管全船，纵然此人并不称职。

===Original===
But the danger was not all over yet. The vessel was now tumbling about in the high swell at the edge of the breakers, the wind had dropped, and to have weighed the anchor would have been to have run great risk of being carried on to the rocks by the rollers. So, as she was safe where she was for the time, I saw it was advisable to wait until the conditions should be more favourable, before shifting our anchorage. The doctor and myself enjoyed our square meal to which we had been looking forward, and then I turned in to sleep, giving orders that I should be called at four in the afternoon.

===Chinese===
然而危险尚未全了过去。船此刻在碎浪边缘的高涌里翻腾颠簸，风已停息，此时起锚，便大有被滚浪掼上礁石之险。好在她暂泊原地还算安稳，我看还是等条件更有利些再换锚地为妥。我和医生美美享用了一路盼望的那顿饭菜，随后我便翻身睡下，吩咐下午四点叫醒我。

===Original===
At four the sea had gone down a good deal and there was a moderate breeze, so I decided to move to a safer berth. We hoisted the sails and, while we were getting the anchor up, I took the precaution, seeing what little sea-room we had, of putting the whaleboat in the water, with a long line fastened to the yacht’s bows, ready to pull her head round and tow her seawards should she not cant in the right direction.

===Chinese===
到了四点，海浪已平息了不少，又起了和缓的风，我便决意移往更稳妥的泊位。我们升起帆；起锚的当口，我鉴于回旋水域之狭小，预先把捕鲸艇放到水里，艇上系一根长绳，牢缚于游艇船首，万一她不肯转向正确的方向，便随时可以拨转船头，把她拖向外海。

===Original===
We got away safely, and the anchor was let go in nineteen fathoms close to where we had brought up on our arrival.

===Chinese===
我们安然脱身，锚抛在十九英寻深处，紧挨着我们初到时停泊的地方。

===Original===
The night was fine, but the surf was still roaring on the beach. The mountains now presented a curious appearance, for our fire had spread up the various arms of the ravine almost to the summit, and there were clusters of lights, as of villages, in all directions, while here and there what appeared to be bonfires were blazing, possibly at spots where several dead trees had fallen together. We began to fear lest the illumination, which must have been visible for leagues out to sea, might attract the attention of passing vessels. A captain would naturally conclude that these fires were the signals of a shipwrecked crew, and therefore go out of his course to render assistance. Luckily this did not happen.

===Chinese===
夜里天色晴好，海滩上的碎浪却依旧咆哮。群山此刻呈现出一派奇景：我们放的那场火，沿着山谷的条条支臂一路烧了上去，几乎直抵山顶；点点火光成团成簇，宛如村落灯火，四面八方皆是；又处处有篝火模样的东西熊熊燃烧，多半是几棵枯树倒在一处的地方。我们开始担心，这片数里格外的海上都必然望得见的火光，会不会引来过往船只的注意。船长们自然会断定这些火是海难船员的求救信号，从而偏出航线前来救援。所幸这样的事并没有发生。

## XIV / 十四

===Original===
We Land the Stores in the Bay

===Chinese===
我们把给养运上湾岸

===Original===
The patience of my men was now to be severely tried. Here before them was the mysterious isle, with all its golden possibilities; but for five days the sea was in far too disturbed a condition to permit of a landing; so they were confined to their floating prison, which rolled and pitched at her anchorage all the while, and gazed with vain desire at the forbidden land.

===Chinese===
我手下人的耐性，如今要经受一场严峻的考验了。那座神秘的岛屿就在眼前，带着一切黄金般的可能；然而一连五天，海面汹涌得太不平静，登岸无从谈起；他们只得困守这座漂浮的牢狱，任她在锚地上不住地横摇纵摆，一面徒然渴慕地遥望那片禁地。

===Original===
It was now that Ted came up to me, as spokesman for the rest of his shipmates in the forecastle, and said that they were all anxious to go on shore in turn, and do their share of digging with the rest of us. It had been part of the original scheme to keep the paid hands﻿—with the exception, perhaps, of the cook﻿—on board the vessel; but as by this time we knew the ways of the *Alerte*, and could handle her with fewer men than when we had started. I decided that an officer and two paid hands would be a sufficient crew while she was lying off the island, and that all the other men could be spared for the work on shore. I therefore acceded to Ted’s request.

===Chinese===
就在这时，特德作为水手舱众伙计的代表来找我，说大家全都盼着能轮番上岸，同我们一起出力挖掘。原定计划里，受雇水手——或许厨子可算例外——本该尽数留在船上；但事到如今，我们已摸熟了「阿勒特号」的脾性，比起航时用更少的人手也能驾驭她。于是我定夺：船泊岛外期间，留一名军官加两名受雇水手充作船员足矣，其余人手尽数可抽去干岸上的活计。我便应允了特德的请求。

===Original===
The men were led to understand that they would be entitled to no share of the proceeds if the treasure were found, though they, of course, knew that, should fortune favour us, a handsome present would be given to them.

===Chinese===
众人也得了明白话：即便寻得宝藏，他们也无权分取一份收益；当然，他们心里有数，一旦时运眷顾我们，必有一份丰厚的馈赠赏到他们头上。

===Original===
The agreement as to the division of the spoil among the gentlemen-adventurers had also to be revised in one respect. It was settled that the shares of those who had abandoned the expedition were to be portioned out among those who remained. By this arrangement each of my companions became nearly twice as rich﻿—in expectations﻿—as when he sailed from England.

===Chinese===
绅士冒险家们之间分赃的协议，也有一处须得修订。议定：中途弃队诸人的份额，摊分给留下来的人。经此一番安排，我的每位同伴——就「期望」而言——比从英格兰启航时几乎阔了一倍。

===Original===
Trinidad is supposed to be outside the limit of the southeast trade-winds, but I think this is doubtful; for, so far as my experience goes, the prevailing winds are from the easterly quarter, and more commonly from the southeast. When the winds are in the west quadrant, and more especially when from the southwest, a heavy sea rises, and landing is rendered altogether impossible. This was our experience for the next few days.

===Chinese===
人们认为特林达迪位于东南信风带的界限之外，我看未必然；据我一己的经验，此地盛行之风出自东面诸向，而以东南为最常见。风入西面诸向时——尤其是西南来风——便涌起大浪，登岸便全然无望。此后数日，我们身受的正是这般。

===Original===
On November 24, there was a high wind from the northwest and a great swell. We were now on a lee shore, and a very dangerous one too; so all was got ready for slipping the anchor and running to the open sea in a moment, should it become necessary to do so. We gave the yacht all her starboard chain﻿—sixty fathoms. We got up the end of the chain, and made it fast to the mainmast in such a way that we could let it go at once. One end of a stout thirty-fathom hawser was attached to the chain, just below the hawsepipe, and to the other end of it we fastened an improvised buoy, made of a breaker and a small bamboo raft. In order to get under way we should now merely have to throw the buoy overboard and cast off the end of the chain from the mast. We could then sail away and leave our moorings behind us.

===Chinese===
11 月 24 日，刮起猛烈的西北风，涌浪滔滔。我们此刻正处下风岸，而且是极危险的下风岸；于是万事备妥，一旦需要，便可立刻弃锚疾驶，逃往外海。我们把游艇右舷的锚链放到了头——整整六十英寻。又把链尾绞上来，设法缚牢在主桅上，好一撒手便能即刻放出。一根三十英寻的结实粗缆，一端系在紧贴锚链筒之下的锚链上，另一端则缚上一只临时扎成的浮标，由一只淡水桶和一张小竹筏拼成。如今要动身，只消把浮标抛下海、再把链尾从桅上解开即可。然后便可扬长驶去，把整套系泊家伙撇在身后。

===Original===
Then we set to work to bend the storm-trysail, a very handy sail, which could be hoisted much more readily than our heavy mainsail. We reefed the foresail, had a storm-jib ready, and housed our topmast. We were now prepared for anything that might turn up.

===Chinese===
接着我们动手装上风暴斜桁帆——一张极为便利的帆，比那面沉重的主帆升起来省事得多。我们把前帆收了缩帆，备妥一张风暴艏帆，又降下了顶桅。至此，无论冒出什么变故，我们都已严阵以待。

===Original===
We were not idle this day, for after making all snug, we got the spades, hydraulic jack, and other tools out of the hold, so as to have them in readiness to put in the boat the moment there was a chance of landing.

===Chinese===
这一天我们也没有闲着：一切收拾妥当之后，我们把铁锹、液压千斤顶和其他工具从货舱里搬了出来，好歹一有登岸的机会，即刻便能装进艇里。

===Original===
Our fire on the mountain blazed away all this night and was not entirely extinguished for six days afterwards.

===Chinese===
我们在山上放的那场火，整整烧了一夜，此后六天都没有完全熄灭。

===Original===
The next day was overcast, and the wind was from the southwest; then it veered to the southward. The sea was higher than on the previous day. The vessel tumbled about a great deal, rolling her scuppers under water, flooding her decks, and running her bowsprit under, all the while. Still, she rode very easily, the great length of heavy chain we had given her acting as a spring. We watched carefully for the first signs of dragging, but the anchor had evidently got a good hold now and she did not budge a foot. In the afternoon the glass fell rapidly and the sky looked very stormy, while the temperature in our saloon fell to 75°, which made us feel quite chilly.

===Chinese===
第二天阴云蔽日，风从西南来，随后又转向南面。海比头一天更高。船颠簸得厉害，一直把排水孔压进水里，甲板时时漫水，艏斜桅也不住往浪里扎。饶是如此，她泊得倒还安稳：我们放给她的那一长串重锚链，恰似一根弹簧，起着缓冲的作用。我们小心提防着走锚的头一丝征兆，但锚这回显然抓得牢靠，她一寸也不曾挪动。午后气压计急落，天色甚是狰狞，舱厅里的温度跌到 75 度（华氏），我们竟觉出相当的寒意。

===Original===
It is probable that this disturbed weather and high sea were the results of a *pampero* raging thousands of miles to the southward of us.

===Chinese===
这番恶劣天气和滔滔巨涌，多半是我们南面数千英里外一场肆虐的帕姆佩罗风所致。

===Original===
On this day we took our dinghy on deck﻿—a dilapidated little boat﻿—and proceeded to stop her leaks, in a novel, but for the time effectual, manner, with plaster of Paris and tar.

===Chinese===
这天我们把小划艇搬上甲板——一条破败的小船——着手给她堵漏，用的是熟石膏和柏油，法子新奇，倒也一时见效。

===Original===
The fish would not be caught while this heavy sea was running, but we secured some sharks and ate their flesh for dinner, to the horror of our black cook, whom I overheard telling his shipmates that he considered it “degrading to eat de meat of de dam shark.”

===Chinese===
这样的大浪天气，鱼是不肯咬钩的，我们却逮住了几条鲨鱼，拿它们的肉当了晚餐，直教我们的黑人厨子骇然；我无意间听见他对同船的伙计说，他觉得「吃那该死的鲨鱼肉，实在有失身份」。

===Original===
November 26.﻿—Same weather, blowing, raining, rolling, and impatient grumbling of men. Even the two amiable blacks, eager to be at work on shore, fretted a bit at the enforced imprisonment on board. They had always been fond of argument, but now the arguments became stormy, and we could hear them laying down the law to each other in the forecastle, while the English sailors sat round them, smoking in silence and listening with amused wonder. One black was a Roman Catholic, the other a Methodist; their discussions were generally theological, and they exchanged vituperations with a fine theological fury. It was grand to hear Theodosius rail at the Pope and call his comrade a heathen idolater, while George would pour the vials of his wrath on the Methodist heretic. These two poor fellows were the greatest friends, but, of course, each was confident that the other was doomed to perdition. When, in the course of one of these controversies, a theologian found himself caught in a dilemma, he would wax impatient and cry, “Oh, chew it!”﻿—an expression I have never heard before﻿—indicating that one has been worsted in argument, but will not allow it, and insists, having had enough of it, on winding up the debate at once.

===Chinese===
11 月 26 日。——天气照旧：刮风，下雨，船摇，人则焦躁地咕哝不休。连那两个和气的黑人——他们一心盼着上岛干活——也因这强加的囚禁生出了几分烦躁。他们俩素来好辩，如今论起来更是火药味十足，我们听得见他们在水手舱里各摆各的教义、互相训诫，英国水手们围坐一圈，闷头抽烟，又惊又乐地听着。一个黑人是天主教徒，另一个是卫理公会教徒；他们的辩题多半属神学，对骂起来义怒勃发，满口神学腔调。听西奥多修斯痛骂教皇、斥他的同伴为拜偶像的异教徒，而乔治把满腔义怒尽数泼向卫理公会异端，实在精彩极了。这两个可怜人是顶顶要好的朋友，可是不消说，两人又都笃信对方注定沉沦。每逢一场论战中哪位神学家自觉落下了风、进退两难，他便按捺不住，嚷一声「噢，嚼它去吧！」——这种说法我从前从未听过——意思是自己辩输了却不肯认账，又实在够了，非要当场收场不可。

===Original===
On the 27th the glass rose, the wind veered to northeast, and the sea moderated; but the surf was still dangerous, and we could see it breaking over a rock sixty feet in height. On this day we sighted two homeward-bound sailing-vessels. During our stay on Trinidad we saw a good many craft, sometimes four or five in a week, all homeward-bounders, for, as I have already explained, it is usual for vessels coming round Cape Horn to make for and sight this island, so as to correct the rate of their chronometers. Few outward-bounders pass it, and it is altogether out of the track of steamers.

===Chinese===
27 日，气压计回升，风转东北，海也平缓了些；但碎浪依然凶险，我们望得见浪头正扑过一块六十英尺高的礁岩。这一天我们望见两条返航的帆船。滞留特林达迪期间，我们见到的船只着实不少，有时一星期多达四五条，全是返航船——缘由我在前面已有交代：绕行合恩角而来的船，惯于驶近并望见此岛，借以校准天文钟的走速。出航的船少有从此经过，这里更完全不在汽船的航线上。

===Original===
On November 28 things looked better, the sea had all gone down. In the morning a few hands pulled off to the pier, where they found the landing perfectly easy, and brought off the coat which the doctor had left on the rock when we had jumped into the sea. My coat could not be found, as it had been washed off by a wave. They also brought off a specimen of a land-crab, which did not seem at all at home on our deck. He was introduced to Master Jacko, our monkey, whose horror at the uncouth apparition was intense. The wise monkey would not get within reach of the crab’s nippers, but, having cleverly driven him into a corner, tried to push his ugly visitor through a scupper into the sea with a bit of firewood.

===Chinese===
11 月 28 日，气象好转，海浪全平了。早晨几名水手划往天然码头，发现登岸易如反掌，还带回了医生那天我们跳海时留在岩上的那件上衣。我的上衣却寻不见了，早被一个浪头卷下了海。他们还带回一只陆蟹标本，这东西在我们甲板上显得浑身不自在。人们把它引见给我们养的猴子——杰科（Jacko）小少爷；这位小少爷见了那副粗野的怪相，骇得魂不附体。这只机灵的猴子死活不肯凑到蟹螯够得着的地方，却伶俐地把客人逼进一个角落，拿起一小块柴火，要把这位其貌不扬的访客从排水孔捅下海去。

===Original===
I must now apologise to Jacko for not having before this introduced him to my readers. He was a delightful little creature that we had purchased on the Praya at Bahia. He was very affectionate, and was free from malice, though, of course, full of mischief. He had a red blanket of his own, which he would carry about with him wherever he went, and, should a few drops of rain fall or spray come on board, he would deftly roll it about him in the fashion of a cloak, with his funny little head just peeping out of the hood. He was very fond of tea, and while we were at sea he took his 4 a.m. cup with the others. As soon as the cook began to lift the boiler of tea from the stove Jacko would give a whistle of delight, clamber up the pantry wall, unhook a pannikin, and walk up with it to be filled, “all de same as a little ole man,” as the cook used to say. It was amusing to see him test the temperature of the tea with his fingers before drinking it. He was a marvellously intelligent and jolly little creature, and is now dwelling happily in a little house on a coconut tree in a plantation near Port-of-Spain. He prefers a West Indian life of warmth and unlimited bananas to an existence in a damp ship on salt junk and biscuit.

===Chinese===
我直到此刻还不曾把杰科引见给诸位读者，得先向它赔个不是。它是我们在巴伊亚的普拉亚买下的一只小家伙，讨人喜欢得很。它极重情义，心里没有半分恶意，当然，淘气也是淘气极了。它有一条自己的红毯子，走到哪儿带到哪儿；天上若飘下几滴雨，或是浪花溅上船来，它便麻利地把毯子往身上一裹，权当斗篷，那滑稽的小脑袋恰从风帽里探出一点。它顶爱喝茶；我们在海上时，凌晨 4 点那一杯，它同大家一样一顿不落。厨子刚把煮茶的大桶从炉子上端起来，杰科便欢哨一声，攀上配膳室的墙壁，摘下一只小铁杯，端端正正走上前去候着斟满——「活脱脱一个小老头儿」，厨子常这么说。看它喝之前先伸指头试试茶的温度，着实好笑。这小东西聪明快活得出奇，如今正在西班牙港（Port-of-Spain）附近一座庄园的椰子树上安居着一间小屋，日子过得快快活活。比起潮乎乎的船上、咸肉硬饼干度日的生涯，它更爱西印度的暖热和吃不尽的香蕉。

===Original===
At noon, as the sea was still smooth, we made our first attempt at landing in Treasure Bay. We put the whaleboat in the water, and loaded her with about a ton of stores, consisting of tinned provisions of various sorts, biscuit, salt beef, the picks, spades, crowbars, wheelbarrows, hydraulic jack, and other tools. We also took in tow a raft constructed of the long bamboos we had brought from Bahia. These we knew would be useful for several purposes.

===Chinese===
正午时分，海面依然平静，我们初次尝试在宝藏湾（Treasure Bay）登岸。我们把捕鲸艇放下水，装上约一吨给养：各色罐头、硬饼干、咸牛肉，外加十字镐、铁锹、撬棍、手推车、液压千斤顶和别的工具。我们又拖上一张筏子，用从巴伊亚带来的长竹扎成，我们知道这东西用处不少。

===Original===
I steered the boat, while the doctor, Powell, Pursell, and two paid hands, took the oars. Having the wind behind us we were not long in crossing the two miles of smoothly heaving sea that lay between us and Southwest Bay. We rounded the point into the bay, and, leaving on our port hand the islet in the middle, we made for the channel which the doctor and myself had surveyed from the mountains. When we came near we found that there were three parallel lines of breakers to be traversed, and, consequently, there was a treble chance of swamping. The surf was much more formidable than we had expected to find it, considering how smooth the sea was outside the bay. The wind was blowing in strong gusts right off shore, over the depression in the mountains at the back of the bay. It drove off the tops of the oncoming waves into great veils of spray, curling over in a contrary direction to the curl of the swell, and bright with shifting rainbows as the sun’s rays fell upon it. The bay presented a most beautiful appearance from the boat, and those who had not seen the pirates’ haunt before uttered exclamations of admiration and wonder. Between the gloomy black mountains on the left and the unearthly-looking dark red walls of Noah’s Ark on the right was a scene in which, flooded with tropical sunlight, earth and ocean vied with each other in vividness of colouring. Directly in front were the great rollers of transparent green, their snowy crests flashing with rainbows; beyond, dazzling golden sands; above, domes of brilliant emerald cleaving the cloudless sky.

===Chinese===
我掌舵，医生、鲍威尔、珀塞尔和两名受雇水手操桨。风从船尾来，我们与西南湾之间那两英里微涌起伏的海面，不多时便划了过去。我们绕过海岬进得湾来，把湾心的小岛让在左舷，直奔医生和我先前从山上勘测过的那道水道。驶近了才发现，须穿过三道平行的碎浪，也就是说，有覆舟的三重风险。湾外海面如此平静，湾里的碎浪却远比我们预想的凶猛。风一阵紧似一阵，正从岸上、从湾后群山的凹口处直吹出海。它把迎面而来的浪头削成大片纱幕似的水沫，卷曲的方向恰与涌浪的卷曲相反，阳光照上去，闪动着变幻不定的虹彩。从艇上望去，这湾景致美得摄人心魄，先前不曾见过海盗巢穴的人们连声惊叹，赞不绝口。左边是黑沉沉的阴郁群山，右边是「诺亚方舟」那暗红色的、恍若非人间的壁墙，其间铺展着一幅图景：热带的阳光漫洒其上，大地与海洋争奇斗艳，各不相让。正前方是一道道碧透的巨浪，雪白的浪峰虹光闪烁；再远处，是晃眼的金色沙滩；头顶上，是鲜绿欲滴的穹丘，劈开万里无云的长天。

===Original===
But this was no time to dwell on the beautiful; we had other matters to consider. The grand rollers with their breaking tops had no charms for us, for we had to get through them﻿—a risky undertaking with a deeply-laden boat.

===Chinese===
但这不是流连美景的时候，我们另有要事待谋。这些顶着碎白浪尖的大滚浪，对我们毫无可爱之处——我们非得从它们中间穿过去不可，对一条满载的小艇来说，这是桩凶险的营生。

===Original===
We discovered afterwards that it is almost impossible to judge from the height of the swell near our anchorage, or from the surf on the pier, whether landing in Southwest Bay is likely to be easy or the reverse. The surf on this sandy beach is governed by a different system of laws to that which prevails on other portions of the coast of Trinidad. Here, curiously enough, there is more surf when the wind is blowing off shore than when it is blowing on. The northeast wind, sweeping in violent gusts down the slopes that back the bay, offers a resistance to the swell rolling in, and piles it into steep walls of water, breaking dangerously. The southeast wind raises a higher swell outside, but, blowing right into this bay, drives the sea down, and the landing becomes comparatively easy. At the anchorage opposite the cascade the contrary is the rule: with a northeast wind blowing off shore the sea is smooth, with a southeast wind the surf increases; but, as I have already stated, it is always smoother there than in Southwest Bay.

===Chinese===
我们后来才发现：想凭锚地附近的涌高、或天然码头上的碎浪，来判断在西南湾登岸的难易，几乎是不可能的。这片沙滩上的碎浪，遵循的是另一套规律，与特林达迪海岸其余地段全然不同。说来奇怪，这里风从岸上往外吹时，碎浪反倒比风从海上往岸吹时更凶。东北风顺着湾后山坡猛扫而下，顶住了滚进湾来的涌浪，把它垒成一道道陡立的水墙，崩裂得惊心动魄。东南风在外海掀起的涌更高，但它径直灌进这湾里，反倒把海面压了下去，登岸便比较容易。瀑布对面的锚地则规律相反：东北风离岸吹时海面平静，东南风一起碎浪便涨；但如我先前所言，那里的海面总是比西南湾平静。

===Original===
The men rested on their oars, and we watched the surf from a safe distance, to discover if there were any chance of picking a favourable opportunity for landing. It would be a disappointing matter if we had to pull our boatload of stores back to the yacht against the wind; so, after a little hesitation, I decided to risk the landing. One must run some risks on such a place as Trinidad, and we might as well commence at once. All in the boat were delighted at the decision.

===Chinese===
桨手们停桨歇息，我们在安全的距离外观察碎浪，看有没有可能觅得一个有利的登岸时机。若要顶着风把这满艇给养划回游艇去，那才叫人丧气；于是踌躇片刻之后，我决意冒险登岸。在特林达迪这等地方，风险总归要担上几分，索性从现在担起。艇中人听了这个决定，个个欢喜。

===Original===
Everyone knows how the ocean swell proceeds in regular rhythm, and how one sees at intervals three greater waves than usual come up, one after the other, to be succeeded by a comparative calm. We took the boat just outside the outer breakers and awaited one of these smoothes. Soon three great waves passed under us, and broke beyond us with terrific force. Now was our time, and we made a dash for it. The long ash oars bent as the men, putting their backs into their work, drove the boat through the sea. Pull away! Pull away! The first row of breakers is passed; then we are safely borne on the top of the second, looking down upon the beach as from a hill. It passes us and breaks. All safe so far. We are close to the beach. Then, behind us, we see a wall of water suddenly rise, curling over. We should simply be rolled over if we tried to back the boat against it, so the men strain at their oars to reach the shore before it. The boat is just touching the sand, the order is given: “All hands overboard and haul her up,” when the sea pours over our heads, filling the boat. The men leap or are washed overboard. One catches hold of the long painter we had provided in view of such an emergency and contrives to reach the shore; then, planting his heels in the sand, he holds on with all his strength, to prevent the boat being swept off into deep water by the receding wave. At first the other hands are out of their depth, but, as the roller recoils, they feel bottom; then, two of us holding on to one side of the boat and two on the other, while the remaining man scrambles on shore to assist the man with the painter, we haul the boat up till she grounds; then we all stand by till the next roller comes on to help us up a bit further. Here it comes! right over our heads, and we are afloat once more. But the two men on shore haul away with all their might, as do the others when they touch bottom, and when the wave recoils it has left us fifty feet higher up the bank, and out of reach of any heavy body of water.

===Chinese===
人人都知道，海洋的涌浪按着均匀的节拍推进，每隔一阵，便有三个比寻常更高的大浪接踵而来，随后是一段相对的平静。我们把艇停在最外一列碎浪之外，静候这样一个间隙。不多时，三个大浪从艇底滚过，在艇前方以骇人的威力轰然碎裂。时机到了，我们奋力一冲。众人弓腰发力，长长的白蜡木桨都压弯了，硬是把艇从浪里拱了出去。划呀！使劲划呀！第一列碎浪闯过去了；随即我们被稳稳托上第二列浪头，居高临下望着海滩，恰似立在山头。浪从艇底过去，碎了。至此一切无恙。我们已贴近海滩。正在这时，只见身后陡然立起一堵水墙，卷着浪头压来。若倒桨硬顶，必被它掀翻无疑，于是众人拼力划桨，抢在它前头奔岸。艇底方一沾沙，口令已下：「全体下船，把艇拖上去！」话音未落，海水已劈头浇下，灌满了艇。众人或纵身跃出，或被浪冲出艇外。一人抓住我们为防此等急难而备下的长艇首索，挣扎着上了岸；随即脚跟深深扎进沙里，拼尽全力攥住不放，唯恐艇被退浪扫回深水。起初其余人等身下无底，待滚浪一退，脚下便踩着了实地；于是这边两人扳住艇的一舷、那边两人扳住另一舷，余下一人爬上岸去，帮着拽艇首索，众人合力把艇往上拖，直到她搁浅坐底；随后人人守在原地，等下一个滚浪上来，再送我们一程。来了！劈头盖脑浇过，我们又浮了起来。岸上两人拼死力拽，其他人一触到底也跟着发力；待浪退去，艇已被留在滩头五十英尺高处，任是大股的水也够不着了。

===Original===
It was lucky for us that ours was a lifeboat with a watertight compartment at either end or we should not have got out of this scrape so well. The boat did not capsize when she filled, neither did she broach to, her head was always direct for the shore. The tide was coming in fast, so we lost no time in getting her safely drawn up. While some hands took out the stores and tools, others baled her out, and, by placing bamboo rollers under her, we dragged her up the steep incline of sand until she was quite out of reach of the sea. We found that we had not lost or damaged any of our stores, so had good reason to congratulate ourselves on our success.

===Chinese===
亏得我们这条是救生艇，首尾各有一个水密隔舱，不然这一关断不能过得这样利索。艇灌满了水，却既未倾覆，也未横身迎浪，艇头始终正对海岸。潮水正急急上涨，我们不敢耽搁，赶紧把她稳稳拖上了高处。一部分人搬出给养和工具，另一部分人舀干艇中的水，又在她底下垫上竹滚杠，沿着陡峭的沙坡往上拖，直拖到大海全然够不着的地方。清点下来，给养一件未失、一件未损，我们大有理由庆幸这次成功。

===Original===
A tot of rum was served to all hands after their exertions, and then we carried all our property up to the spot we had selected for our camp﻿—a plateau of sand and earth opposite the mouth of the ravine.

===Chinese===
一场劳碌过后，每人发了一小杯朗姆酒，随后我们把全部家当搬上选定作营地的那处地方——裂谷谷口对面的一片沙土台地。

===Original===
Then, as all were, of course, anxious to see the supposed hiding-place of the treasure, the doctor and myself took them to it. On ascending the gully somewhat higher than we had gone on our previous visit we discovered two or three small pools of inferior water. But the supply was insufficient, even after the recent heavy rains; so it was evident that, unless we found some other source, our condensing apparatus would not have been brought in vain. There was, fortunately, an abundance of fuel in the neighbourhood, for the dead trees were strewed over all the hillside.

===Chinese===
接着，众人自然都急于一睹那据信藏宝的所在，医生和我便领他们去了。沿着沟壑上行，比我们上次来时走得略高些，我们发现了两三小汪劣质积水。可即便下了近来这几场大雨，水量仍旧不足；足见倘若寻不着别的水源，我们带来的蒸馏器便决不至于白带。所幸近旁燃料充裕，枯树撒满了整面山坡。

===Original===
We had not brought off any of the tents, but, with a good fire and plenty to eat, drink, and smoke, there would be little hardship in sleeping out; and the doctor and Powell volunteered to stay on shore, while I went back to the yacht. It was my intention to return, if possible, on the following day, with the tents and other stores, and to then leave a working-party on the island. We might, of course, on the other hand, be prevented by a heavy sea from landing again for a week or more; so we bade our comrades an affectionate farewell, and enjoined them not to be lazy, but to dig away until they saw us again﻿—a quite unnecessary suggestion, for they were very keen to begin work.

===Chinese===
帐篷我们一顶也没有带来，不过有旺旺的篝火，有吃有喝有烟抽，露宿也算不得什么苦处；医生和鲍威尔自告奋勇留守岸上，我便回游艇去。我打算翌日若得可能，便带着帐篷和其他给养回来，然后留一拨人在岛上做工。当然，也可能大风浪一连一星期或更久不许我们再登岸；于是我们与同伴们依依作别，叮嘱他们休要偷懒，只管埋头挖下去，直到再见我们——这叮嘱纯属多余，他们早已摩拳擦掌，急欲动工了。

===Original===
Taking with me Pursell and the paid hands, we hauled the boat down to the beach; we dragged her into the water quickly, just as one big roller was recoiling, jumped in and pulled hard out to sea. We shipped a little water at the second line of breakers, and were then in safety.

===Chinese===
我带上珀塞尔和受雇水手们，把艇拖下海滩；恰逢一个大滚浪退走，我们飞快把她推进水里，跳进去奋力向外海划去。穿过第二列碎浪时溅进了点海水，随后便安然无恙了。

===Original===
We soon found, as we pulled back to the yacht, that our boat had sprung a leak, for the water was pouring in fast through her bottom, so that we had to stop and bale occasionally. She was an excellent sea-boat, but lightly built, and her bump on the sands had done her no good.

===Chinese===
往回划向游艇的路上，我们很快发现艇底裂了缝漏水，海水正从底板汩汩直灌，只得时不时停下来舀一舀。她原是条顶好的海上用艇，只是造得单薄，在沙滩上那一下磕碰，没给她落下什么好处。

## XV / 十五

===Original===
Our Camp

===Chinese===
我们的营地

===Original===
We hoisted our leaky lifeboat into the davits when we got on board, intending to repair her on the following morning.

===Chinese===
回到船上，我们把漏水的救生艇吊上艇架，打算第二天早晨修理她。

===Original===
During the night fierce gusts blew down the ravine from the northeast, and black masses of cloud were constantly sweeping across the mountains. The wind howled as it does in a wintry gale on the North Sea, and, to all appearance, a heavy storm was raging. Still, it was quite smooth at our anchorage under the lee of the island, and we noticed that seawards the sky looked fine enough, and the clouds were travelling at no great pace. The storm, in fact, was entirely local, and was limited to the islet and its immediate neighbourhood. We afterwards became quite accustomed to these harmless gales, which had a habit of springing up at sunset.

===Chinese===
夜里，猛烈的阵风顺着裂谷从东北方刮下来，一团团乌云不断扫过群山。风声呼啸，一如北海冬日风暴里的光景，看那阵仗，俨然一场狂风暴雨正在肆虐。然而在我们这处背倚岛屿下风的锚地，海面却相当平静；我们又留意到，朝海的方向望去，天空显得足够晴朗，云也行得不快。这场风暴其实纯属局部，只波及这个小岛及其近旁一带。此后我们对这类无害的狂风便习以为常了——它们惯于在日落时分骤然而起。

===Original===
Trinidad, in consequence of the loftiness of its mountains, can boast of a climate of its own. It is subject to miniature cyclones, whose influence does not extend a mile from the shore, and which, therefore, cannot raise a heavy sea. We were sometimes riding with straining chain to a wind of hurricane force, when we could see a vessel a league or so from the land making no progress, her canvas shaking in the calm; and, however fine it might be outside, the clouds would collect upon the peaks in ominous torn masses, that whirled along as if impelled by a terrific blast, and which looked very alarming until we came to understand the innocence of the phenomenon. We also found that the landing was often the most perilous on clear, windless days, when no clouds crowned the mountains.

===Chinese===
特林达迪因山势高峻，自有一套独有的气候。岛上常起一种小型的气旋，其影响不出海岸一英里，因而掀不起大浪。有时我们绷紧锚链，顶着一场飓风级的大风，却望得见离岸一里格光景的一条船寸步难进，帆篷在无风里簌簌抖动；而且无论外海天气多么晴好，云总会聚上峰顶，结成一片片不祥的碎块，仿佛被一股骇人的狂飙驱赶着飞旋——在我们明白这现象纯属无害之前，它看去着实吓人。我们还发现，登岸往往在晴朗无风、群山不戴云冠的日子最为凶险。

===Original===
These storms were, however, a nuisance to us; for the squalls would strike the yacht with great force, so that she strained at her chain and was likely to drag; consequently the officer in charge was unable to enjoy an undisturbed night’s rest, but was in a state of constant anxiety for the vessel, and was often brought on deck by the turmoil to satisfy himself that all was going well.

===Chinese===
不过，这些风暴于我们也是桩烦扰；因为阵风会大力扑击游艇，逼得她绷紧锚链，随时可能走锚；因此负责的军官夜里休想安生，时刻为船悬着心，常常被这番喧嚣赶上甲板，亲眼验看一切无恙才肯放心。

===Original===
The next day, November 29, was fine, the wind being still from the northeast. There was even less swell than on the previous day, so we saw that no time must be lost in landing more stores. A neglected opportunity on Trinidad might mean a month’s delay.

===Chinese===
第二天，11 月 29 日，天气晴好，风仍从东北来。涌浪比头一天更小了，我们明白，再运一批给养上岸的事刻不容缓。在特林达迪，错失一次机会，可能就意味着耽搁一个月。

===Original===
We examined the boat, and found that she had started a plank, but that the damage was slight and could be easily repaired. A few copper nails, some cotton thrust between the seams with a knife, and a little marine glue, made her right again; and, after breakfast, she put off to Treasure Bay with a miscellaneous cargo﻿—the tents, a barrel of flour, wire-fencing, the blankets and baggage for the shore-party, etc.; but we did not venture to put nearly so heavy a weight into her as on the previous day.

===Chinese===
我们检查了小艇，发现有一块船板松了榫，好在损毁轻微，容易修理。几枚铜钉、用刀塞进板缝的些许填絮，再加一点船用胶，她便完好如初了。早饭过后，她载着一船杂货驶往宝藏湾——帐篷、一桶面粉、铁丝网、留给岸上队伍的毯子和行李等等；不过我们不敢再像头一天那样，往她肚里装那么重的分量了。

===Original===
The surf in the bay was no longer dangerous, and, though water was shipped, all was landed without accident. At midday the boat returned to the yacht, was reloaded, and another successful disembarkation was effected. This put us in very good spirits. We had succeeded in overcoming the difficulties that had caused previous expeditions to fail, and had now got on shore all that was absolutely necessary for carrying on the digging for some time to come. The doctor, Pursell, Powell, and Ted Milner were left on shore for the night, and the boat returned to the yacht.

===Chinese===
湾里的碎浪已不凶险，虽然进了些水，全部物资都安然无恙地卸上了岸。正午，小艇回到游艇，重新装载，又完成了一次顺利的登陆。这教我们士气大振。先前几次远征折戟沉沙的那些难关，我们已成功闯过，今后一段时间挖掘所需的家当，如今已尽数运抵岸上。医生、珀塞尔、鲍威尔和特德·米尔纳当晚留在岸上，小艇返回了游艇。

===Original===
The next day, November 30, was the first on which we divided ourselves definitely into two parties, the working-gang on shore and a crew of three to take charge of the yacht. I had talked our plans over on the previous day with my sole officer, our medico-mate, and we came to the conclusion that it would be advisable for me to stay on board for the first fortnight, at least; for we did not know as yet whether it would be safe to remain at anchor for any length of time, or what steps might become necessary in order to ensure the safety of the vessel; and, until such knowledge had been gained by experience of the conditions of the place, it was right that I should undertake the responsibility of looking after the yacht.

===Chinese===
第二天，11 月 30 日，是我们正式分成两拨的头一天：一拨是岸上的工作队，一拨是照管游艇的三人船员。前一天，我已同我唯一的军官——我们那位医生兼大副——把计划细细商议过，得出结论：头两个星期，至少头两个星期，以我留在船上为宜；因为久留锚地究竟是否安全、要保游艇无恙须采取什么步骤，我们都还没有底；而在凭此间实情的经验摸清这些之前，由我来承担照看游艇的责任，是理所应当的。

===Original===
So, on this morning, I went on shore for the last time, before settling down to my fortnight’s watch. We took another cargo of stores in the boat, and landed without difficulty. This long spell of smooth sea was a most fortunate occurrence for us.

===Chinese===
于是这天早晨，我在安顿下来开始那两星期的值守之前，最后一回上了岸。我们又往艇里装了一船给养，毫无困难地登了岸。这样长一段时间的平稳海面，于我们实在是天大的幸事。

===Original===
On landing I found that the shore-party had been hard at work. They had arranged the camp﻿—and very snug it looked. Two ridge tents had been placed side by side, to be occupied by the gentlemen-volunteers, two in each; while a short way off was a larger tent, constructed of our racing spinnaker and the quarterdeck awning supported by bamboos. This was our dining-room and kitchen, and also served as sleeping quarters for the paid hands. At one end of it was an elegant dining-table﻿—planks from the deck of some old wreck, supported by one of Mr. A﻿—﻿—’s wheelbarrows which had been found in the ravine. A few campstools and barrels served as chairs, and the arrangements generally were almost luxurious.

===Chinese===
一上岸，我便发现岸上的队伍干得正欢。营地已经布置停当——看去十分安适。两顶人字形帐篷并排支着，归绅士志愿队员居住，每顶两人；不远处还有一顶较大的帐篷，是用我们比赛用的大三角帆和后甲板天篷、以竹竿撑起来的。这便是我们的饭厅兼厨房，也充作受雇水手的寝处。帐篷一头摆着一张雅致的餐桌——桌面是某条旧沉船甲板上拆下的木板，支架则是从裂谷里寻回的一辆A——先生的手推车。几只折叠凳和几只桶权当座椅，整体布置几乎称得上奢华了。

===Original===
Many improvements were made to the camp during our stay in Trinidad, and at last it became a comfortable little village. A conspicuous object near the tents was the condensing apparatus. Later on, the cooking was all done out of doors, a neat oven having been constructed of stones and plaster of Paris. The plaster of Paris had formed part of the taxidermist’s stores, but, little used for its original purpose, it was found to be of much service in the way of cement.

===Chinese===
滞留特林达迪期间，营地屡经改进，末了竟成了一座舒舒服服的小村落。帐篷近旁一件显眼的东西，便是那套蒸馏器。后来做饭全挪到了露天，用石头和熟石膏砌起了一座齐整的炉灶。这熟石膏原是标本师行装里的一份，本来的用途没怎么用上，倒发现它拿来做水泥大有用场。

===Original===
A list of all that we landed on the shore of Southwest Bay would be a long one. There was, at the very least, eight tons weight in all. I need not say that the cook was well provided with culinary apparatus, and that such articles as paraffin lamps for the tents, a library of books, fishing lines and hooks, and carpenter’s tools had not been forgotten﻿—our camp, in short, was fully furnished with everything that could be required.

===Chinese===
若把我们在西南湾岸上卸下的东西开一张清单，那要很长。总计至少有八吨之重。不必说，厨子的炊具家什一应俱全，而诸如帐篷用的煤油灯、一批藏书、钓线和钓钩、木工工具之类的物件，也一件不曾忘带——总之，我们这座营地，凡用得着的家什，无不齐备。

===Original===
The doctor and myself discussed the scheme of work on shore, and, when all was settled we launched the boat again and pulled off to the yacht. It was decided that the shore-party should keep the whaleboat﻿—in the first place, because the crew on board would be insufficient to man her, and, secondly, because it was only right and prudent to leave a boat on the island in case of any accident happening to the yacht. It would be easy for the working-party to pull off, if necessary, and intercept a passing vessel. The dilapidated dinghy was left on board for our use.

===Chinese===
医生和我商量了岸上的工作章程，一切议定之后，我们重新放下小艇，划回游艇。议定捕鲸艇归岸上的队伍使用：其一，船上的人手不足以驾驭她；其二，万一游艇出了什么意外，岛上留一条艇才是正理，也是稳妥之计。必要时，工作队尽可划出海去，拦截过往的船只。那条破败的小划艇则留在船上，归我们使用。

===Original===
The hands who had come off in the boat dined on board, and then the doctor, taking with him those who were going to stay on shore, pulled back to the bay, to commence his duties as Governor of Trinidad, leaving me with my two hands, Wright, and the coloured man Spanner. And a very good governor the doctor proved too, as I discovered when I next went on shore and saw the work that had been got through. He kept up a discipline quite strict enough for all practical purposes. He did more work than anyone else himself, being physically the strongest man of us all, and he superintended all the operations with great skill and judgment. The control could not have been left in better hands, and he was well backed up by his comrades. There was hard work done on that island, considerable hardships were undergone, there was often dangerous landing and beaching of boats, and all was carried on under a vertical sun on one of the hottest and most depressing spots on earth. Great credit is due to the doctor and the others who worked so hard and with such pluck and cheerful zeal, and the ungenerous remarks of the one discontented volunteer we had left﻿—a man who did not do his share of work either at sea or on shore, but who did far more than his share of criticism and faultfinding﻿—can only reflect upon himself. As he has favoured the world with his sneers through the medium of the papers, I feel bound to say this much.

===Chinese===
乘艇过来的那几个水手在船上用了饭，随后医生带上要留岸的人，划回湾里去就任他的特林达迪总督了，把我同我那两名水手——赖特和那个有色人种斯潘纳——留在船上。医生也确实证明是位好总督，这在我下一次上岸、看见已完成的工作量时便知道了。他维持的纪律，就一切实际用途而言足够严格。他自己干的活比谁都多——论体格，他是我们中间最强壮的一个——督导各项作业，又极见本事和分寸。这份统辖之权，再没有比交到他手里更妥当的了，同伴们也倾力辅佐他。在那座岛上，活计干得艰苦，罪也受得不少，登岸和抢滩常常冒着风险，而这一切都是在烈日垂直暴晒之下、在地球上最酷热最难熬的去处之一里进行的。医生和其他诸君干得那样卖力，又有那般胆气与欣然的热情，实在功不可没；而我们留下的那位心怀不满的志愿队员所说的小气话——此人在海上岸上都不曾干过他名下的那份活，指摘与挑刺却远超他名下的份额——只能折损他自己的体面。他既已在报刊上向世人赐下了他的冷嘲，我觉得有必要说上这几句。

===Original===
The doctor remained and worked hard on the island during the whole time that our operations were being carried on, as did Powell and Pursell, and they, with the paid hands, who relieved each other at intervals, practically did all the digging. I was on shore for one fortnight only, as will appear in the course of this narrative. I had, consequently, but a very small share of the hard work and of roughing it, for the life on board ship was incomparably more comfortable and easy than the life on shore. Our critical volunteer also only passed about two weeks, of not arduous work, on the island; for the rest of the time he was on the yacht.

===Chinese===
我们的工程进行的全部期间，医生始终留在岛上苦干，鲍威尔和珀塞尔亦然；他们几位连同轮番替换的受雇水手，实际包下了全部挖掘。我在岸上统共只待了两个星期，后文自有交代。因此，那份苦工、那份磨难，我分担的实在微薄，因为船上的日子，比岸上不知舒服安逸多少倍。我们那位好挑剔的志愿队员，在岛上也不过待了约莫两个星期，干的还算不上辛苦活计；其余时日，他都在游艇上。

===Original===
This night we had another local storm, but by now we were getting accustomed to this.

===Chinese===
这天夜里又来了一场局部风暴，不过到这时，我们已渐渐见惯不怪了。

===Original===
Shortly after dawn on the following morning, Sunday, December 1, I saw, to my surprise, the whaleboat rounding the point. She came alongside, and the doctor, who was in charge of her boarded us. Seeing that there was very little surf in Southwest Bay, he had rightly taken the opportunity of putting off for another cargo of stores. Among other articles, he carried away some large coconut mats we had purchased at Bahia, and which, when laid on the sandy floor of the tents, would make things more comfortable. He also took off the heavy boiler and receiving tank of the condensing apparatus, which could only be landed on a favourable day such as this was. Having loaded the boat, he left us again.

===Chinese===
次日是星期日，12 月 1 日，破晓后不久，我惊讶地看见捕鲸艇绕过海岬驶来。她靠拢船舷，掌管她的医生上了我们的船。他见西南湾碎浪甚小，便不失时机地又来装一船给养，这一步走得没错。各色物件之中，他载走了几张我们在巴伊亚买的大椰席，铺在帐篷的沙地上，可以舒服不少。他还搬走了蒸馏器那沉重的大锅炉和承液桶——非得赶上这样一个好日子，它们才上得了岸。装好艇，他便又离我们而去。

===Original===
We had now taken so much weight out of the yacht that she was high out of the water, and might possibly prove somewhat cranky under canvas. So, after dinner, I took the two men off with me in the dinghy, for the purpose of fetching some heavy stones from the beach, to put in our hold in the place of all the tools we had taken out. First we pulled to the pier, where we landed without the slightest difficulty. Wright, while wandering about the beach, came across the last object one would expect to find on a desert island﻿—a rather smart lady’s straw-hat, so far as my judgment goes, of modern fashion. It had, probably, been blown off some fair head on a passenger steamer. The gallant gentlemen-adventurers, when they heard of this discovery, proposed that it should be stuck on a pole in the middle of the camp, to remind them of home and beauty.

===Chinese===
我们从游艇上卸下的重量如此之多，她已高高浮出水面，扬起帆来或许会有些失稳易倾。于是正餐过后，我带上那两个人乘小划艇出海，去海滩运些沉重的石头回来，装进货舱，顶替我们搬走的那些工具。我们先划到天然码头，毫不费力地上了岸。赖特在海滩上闲逛时，撞见了一样人们在荒岛上最意想不到的东西——一顶颇为俏丽的女式草帽，据我判断，还是时新的式样。它多半是从某艘客轮上某位俏丽的头顶上刮下来的。豪情雅意的绅士冒险家们听闻这一发现，提议把它插在营地中央的一根竿子上，好教他们惦念着家乡与美人。

===Original===
Finding that there were no suitable stones near this beach, we got in the boat again and rowed to West Bay, to see if we should have better luck there. Three islets lie off the east side of the Ness. We found that the narrow deep-water channel between these and the cape could be taken with safety on a fine day like this. As a rule, this channel is impracticable, for the ocean swell penetrating it produces a great commotion, the sea being dashed with violence from the cliffs on one side to those on the other, so that the entire channel presents the appearance of a boiling cauldron; and, even on this quiet day, we had to keep the boat carefully in the middle, for the waves leapt high up the rocky walls with a loud noise, which was repeated in manifold echoes by the crags above. When we were in the passage between the third islet and the shore the scene before us was most impressive. The black cliffs rose perpendicularly on either side of us, about thirty feet apart, casting a profound shade on the heaving water, so that it looked like ink beneath us; and between these cliffs, as through a dark tunnel, we saw the sunlit waters and shores of West Bay. The mountains that lay to the back of it were barren and of bold outline, great pinnacles of rock dominating huge landslips that slope to the shingle-beach. We could distinguish the familiar forms of the Sugarloaf and Noah’s Ark towering over the depressions of the hills.

===Chinese===
发现这片海滩近旁没有合用的石头，我们重新上艇，划往西湾，看运气会不会好些。内斯岬东侧的外海躺着三座小岛。我们发现，在这样一个晴日，小岛与海岬之间那条狭窄的深水水道可以放心通过。平日里这条水道无法通行，因为钻进来的外海涌浪会搅起一场大乱，海水从一侧峭壁猛撞到另一侧峭壁，整条水道便如一口滚沸的大锅；即便在这样安静的日子，我们也须把艇小心保持在水道正中，因为浪头轰然扑上石壁老高，上方的嶙峋峭岩又把这声响化作重重回音。行到第三座小岛与海岸之间的水路时，眼前的景象最为动人心魄。黑色峭壁在我们两侧笔直拔起，相距约三十英尺，在起伏的水面上投下浓重的阴影，我们脚下的水色黑得如墨；而在两壁之间，仿佛穿过一条幽暗的隧道，我们望见了西湾那阳光灿然的水面与海岸。湾后的群山荒秃而轮廓雄劲，巨大的岩峰俯临着缓缓斜向砾石海滩的大片滑坡。糖面包山和「诺亚方舟」那熟悉的身影，我们也辨认得出，正高高耸出于群山的凹伏之上。

===Original===
At the farther end of the bay we found a suitable place for getting stones. Here a rocky shelf formed a sort of jetty. George leapt on shore and brought down the stones, while Wright, sitting in the stern, took them from him, and placed them at the bottom of the boat, while I backed in towards the jetty and pulled out again between the waves; for there was sufficient sea to do damage if proper caution was not observed. Having taken on board about half a ton of large heavy stones, we returned to the yacht and stowed them under the cabin-floor.

===Chinese===
在湾的尽头，我们寻到一处合适的取石之地。那里一道石台探出海面，恰似一座小码头。乔治跃上岸去搬石头，赖特坐在艇尾，接过他递来的，一块块码进艇底；我则驾着艇倒退凑近石台，又在浪与浪之间划将出来——因为海面颇有些浪头，稍有疏忽便会闯祸。装上约莫半吨又大又重的石头，我们返回游艇，把它们收进舱底地板之下。

===Original===
On the following morning, December 2, the doctor came off again in the lifeboat, and carried off another moderate load of stores. He reported that on the previous day, being Sunday, he had given all hands a holiday on his return to the shore, and that they had passed the day in exploring the neighbourhood of Treasure Bay. They came across some more tent poles and picks left by Mr. A﻿—﻿—’s party. They also made one very curious discovery﻿—a quantity of broken pottery, lying in a little rocky ravine at a considerable height above the shore. All this was of Oriental manufacture. Some was of unglazed earthenware, some of glazed china﻿—the remains of what appeared to have been water-jars and punch-bowls. There were also some broken case-bottles of glass, oxidised and brittle from long exposure. The bowls proved to be of Blue Dragon china, about a hundred years old, and, therefore, of some value to the connoisseur.

===Chinese===
次日早晨，12 月 2 日，医生又乘救生艇过来，运走了又一批数量可观的给养。他报告说，前一天是星期日，他回到岸上后给全体放了假，众人便拿这一天去探察宝藏湾左近。他们又撞见几根帐篷杆和几把十字镐，是A——先生那队人留下的。他们还有一项非常奇特的发现——大量碎裂的陶瓷器，躺在一道离岸颇高的小石谷里。这些全是东方烧造之物：有的是不上釉的陶器，有的是上了釉的瓷器——看那残片，原先像是水罐和潘趣酒碗。还有几只破碎的玻璃方酒瓶，久经风霜，已经氧化发脆。那些碗经鉴别竟是青龙瓷（Blue Dragon），约有百年之谱，因而在行家眼里颇有几分价值。

===Original===
Pottery of this description had certainly not formed part of the equipment of Mr. A﻿—﻿—’s, or of any other of the treasure-hunting expeditions. Could these be relics of the pirates’ booty﻿—articles they had thrown away as being of no value to them when they buried the rest of the treasure? It was, certainly, difficult to account for the presence of old blue china on a barren hillside of Trinidad. It has been suggested by an old sea captain that an East Indiaman may have been wrecked here many years ago, and that her crew had contrived to reach the shore with provisions and other property, for bowls of the same description as those of which these fragments had formed part were commonly used by the Malay sailors to eat their curry in.

===Chinese===
这等陶瓷器物，断不会是A——先生行装里之物，也不属于其他任何一支寻宝远征队。莫非这些是海盗赃物的遗存——是他们埋藏其余财宝时，嫌没有价值而随手丢弃的东西？总之，要在特林达迪一片荒秃的山坡上解释这批旧青花瓷器的来历，确实颇费思量。有一位老船长提出：多年前或许有一条东印度商船在此失事，船员们带着口粮和其他财物设法上了岸——因为这些残片所属的那类碗，正是马来水手们平日吃咖喱用的。

===Original===
The doctor soon left me, and hurried back with his boat’s crew to the camp, for the sea was rising, the glass had been falling for twenty-four hours, and the sky had a stormy appearance, not only over the mountains, but on the sea-horizon as well.

===Chinese===
医生不久便向我告辞，带着他的艇员匆匆赶回营地，因为海浪正在上涨，气压计已连落二十四小时，天色看去颇有风暴之意——不光是群山上空，连海上天际也是如此。

===Original===
These signs of foul weather did not deceive us, for it now blew hard from the southeast for several days, and the sea was so rough that we were unable to launch the dinghy, while, on the other hand, it was impossible to put out from the bay in the whaleboat. All communication was, therefore, cut off between the yacht and the shore for six days, and we could not even see each other during this time, as two capes stretched out between us.

===Chinese===
这些坏天气的征兆没有骗我们：此后数日，东南风刮得猛烈，海况恶劣到我们放不下小划艇，而另一头，捕鲸艇也无法从湾里驶出。于是游艇与岸上的交通断绝了整整六天；这段日子里，我们连彼此的面也见不着，因为有两道海岬横伸在我们中间。

===Original===
It was fortunate that we had landed such an ample supply of stores while the weather was fine.

===Chinese===
幸而天气晴好时，我们已把如此充裕的给养运上了岸。

===Original===
We had rather an uncomfortable time of it on board for the next few days. For a good part of the time the wind was blowing with the force of a gale, and it howled and whistled among the crags in a dreadful fashion, while the surf thundered at the base of the cliffs. The wind being southeast was parallel to this portion of the coast; so we were scarcely, if at all, protected by the island. A great swell rolled up, travelling in the same direction as the wind. But as violent squalls occasionally rushed down the ravines at right angles to the true wind, we were blown round by them, so that we were riding broadside on to the sea, rolling scuppers under in the trough of it, pitching the whole bowsprit in at one moment and thumping our counter on to the water the next.

===Chinese===
此后几天，我们在船上过得颇不自在。有大半时间，风刮得堪比狂风，在岩嶂间呼啸尖鸣，凄厉骇人，碎浪在峭壁脚下雷鸣般轰响。这股东南风与这段海岸的走向平行，岛屿几乎遮蔽不了我们。大涌滚滚而来，与风同向。但猛烈的阵风偶尔会顺着裂谷直冲而下，与真风成直角，把我们吹得团团打转，于是我们横身迎着海面系泊，在波谷里把排水孔都摇进水中，一时整个艏斜桅扎进浪里，一时又拿船尾砰地拍在水面上。

===Original===
Things looked so bad on December 4 that I was thinking of slipping the anchor and putting to sea, but, as the vessel did not appear to be straining herself, I held on. Our dinghy was dipping into the sea as we rolled, so we took it from the davits and secured it on deck.

===Chinese===
12 月 4 日，情势看去十分凶险，我一度想着弃锚出海，但见船身似乎并未吃力受扭，便咬牙坚持住了。我们横摇时，小划艇直往海里浸水，我们便把它从艇架上取下，缚牢在甲板上。

===Original===
We had now ample leisure to study the meteorology of Trinidad. The rains were heavy during this stormy period and the cascade swelled visibly. I do not think this island is subject to drought; for, notwithstanding that this﻿—the summer﻿—was the dry season here, scarcely a day passed without a shower during our long stay. In the winter season this is, to judge from the logs of passing vessels, a very rainy spot. The glass never fell below thirty inches while we were here, and generally stood at about thirty and two-tenths. The temperature in the shade on board averaged about eighty. In the tents on shore it was far hotter. The sunsets are often very fine on Trinidad, of wild and stormy appearance and full of vivid colouring; these indicate fine weather. The boisterous southwest winds, extensions of River Plate *pamperos*, are heralded by clear blue skies.

===Chinese===
我们如今有的是闲工夫，来研究特林达迪的气象。这段风暴期间雨下得很大，那道瀑布眼见着涨了起来。我不认为这座岛屿会闹旱灾；因为此刻——正值夏季——虽是此地的旱季，我们盘桓的漫长日子里，几乎没有一天不飘一阵阵雨。到了冬季，据过往船只的航海日志判断，这里又是个非常多雨的去处。我们在此期间，气压计从未跌破三十英寸，一般停在三十点二英寸上下。船上荫处的温度平均约八十度（华氏）。岸上的帐篷里则热得多。特林达迪的日落往往极美，一派狂野的风暴气象，满目鲜明的色彩；这预示着好天气。而那西南劲风——拉普拉塔河帕姆佩罗风（pamperos）延展开来的一脉——到来之前，先兆却是晴朗的蓝天。

===Original===
We three now imprisoned on the yacht occupied our time in tidying her up, and making all necessary repairs in the sails and gear generally. We occasionally knocked down some birds as they flew over us. Some would coolly perch on our davits and stare at us very rudely, to the great indignation of Jacko, who swore at them in his own language. It was curious to watch the birds fly far out to sea each morning for their day’s fishing, the air full of their shrill and melancholy cries, and return again in the evening. It was invariably while starting at daybreak that they called on the yacht. While going home in the evening they had their business to attend to. It was then that they carried food to their young﻿—fluffy balls of insatiable appetite, which, I am afraid, had sometimes to go to bed supperless; for the anxious mothers are often robbed of their hard-earned fish by the cruel pirates who are perpetually hovering round this island.

===Chinese===
我们三个如今囚在游艇上，便拿收拾船只、修整帆篷索具诸般必要的活计来消磨时光。偶有飞鸟从头顶掠过，我们便打下来几只。有的竟大大咧咧停上我们的艇架，极不客气地瞪着我们，直教杰科愤慨不已，用它自己的语言冲它们咒骂。清早看群鸟远远飞出海去打一天的鱼，空中满是尖利而凄凉的啼声，傍晚又成群归来，煞是有趣。它们来拜访游艇，总不外乎破晓启程的时候；傍晚归家途中，它们自有正事要办——正是那时候，它们给雏鸟捎去食物。那些毛茸茸的小家伙胃口永不餍足，恐怕有时还得上床饿肚子，因为焦急的母亲们辛苦挣来的鱼，常常叫那些残忍的海盗抢了去——这帮海盗永远绕着这座岛盘旋。

===Original===
These pirates are the frigate or man-of-war birds. They do not fish themselves, but attack the honest fishers in midair, and compel them to surrender what they have caught. The frigate-bird is of the orthodox piratical colour﻿—black﻿—but has a vermilion beak and a few white patches on its throat. It has a forked tail, and wings of extraordinary length in proportion to its body, their spread sometimes attaining, it is said, as much as fifteen feet.

===Chinese===
这些海盗便是军舰鸟（frigate bird），又名战舰鸟。它们自己不捕鱼，却在半空袭击本分的捕鱼者，逼它们交出猎获。军舰鸟生就海盗的正宗颜色——黑色——只是喙作朱红，喉间缀着几点白斑。它有一条叉形的尾巴，双翼相对于躯体长得惊人，据说展开来可达十五英尺之多。

===Original===
There are other pirates here as well, of a meaner description, who, being able to fish for themselves, have no excuse for their crimes; whereas the frigate-bird is unable to skim the sea after fish. Should he touch the water he cannot make use of his unwieldy wings and flounders helplessly about until he becomes the prey of sharks.

===Chinese===
这里还有别的海盗，品流更为卑下：它们分明自己捕得鱼，罪行便无可开脱；军舰鸟却不然，贴着海面掠飞捉鱼它是办不到的——一旦碰着水面，那双笨重的翅膀便再施展不开，只能无助地扑腾，直到沦为鲨鱼的口中食。

===Original===
But these other robbers have taken to dishonest ways from sheer laziness and lack of principle. Their favourite method is to seize a smaller fisher by the throat, and hold him under water until he is half drowned and has to disgorge his fish. Sometimes two or three plucky little birds will assist a neighbour in resisting the big bully, and often drive him off discomfited. We witnessed several most exciting combats of this description.

===Chinese===
但这另一伙强盗走上不法之路，纯是出于懒惰和无行。它们惯用的手法，是咬住一只较小的捕鱼鸟的喉咙，把它按在水里呛得半死，逼它吐出鱼来。有时两三只有胆气的小鸟会帮着邻鸟抵抗那大恶霸，还常把杀得它铩羽而退。这样的鏖战我们目睹了好几场，回回惊心动魄。

===Original===
We skinned the birds we killed, and I have brought these specimens home with me. Of fish we now caught plenty. We salted and sun-dried some, but these were not a great success, and had a rank flavour in consequence of their oily nature.

===Chinese===
打下来的鸟我们剥制成皮，这些标本我已带回了家。鱼呢，我们如今钓得很多。我们腌了些、也晒了些，成效却不怎么样，因为鱼肉多油，带着一股浓重的腥气。

## XVI / 十六

===Original===
Discoveries in Southwest Bay

===Chinese===
西南湾的种种发现

===Original===
At last, on December 7, communication between the yacht and the shore was resumed; for the wind and sea had greatly moderated, and the doctor was enabled to come off to us at midday, with four volunteers and paid hands. They had been labouring hard with pick and shovel, and looked like it too. Digging into the volcanic soil of Trinidad soon takes all superfluous flesh off. Indeed, led on by the energetic doctor, they had worked harder, perhaps, than white men should in such a climate, and had a stale overstrained appearance, while they admitted that they felt somewhat slack.

===Chinese===
终于，到 12 月 7 日，游艇与岸上的交通恢复了；因为风浪已大为平息，医生得以在正午带着四名志愿队员和几名受雇水手到船上来。他们一直抡着十字镐和铁锹苦干，模样也着实像是干过苦工的。在特林达迪的火山土里刨掘，用不了多久便能把一身多余的肉都削下来。的确，在那位精力充沛的医生的带动下，他们干的活计，恐怕已超过了白人在这种气候下应有的限度，个个显出一副疲沓透支的神色，自己也承认觉得有些发乏。

===Original===
They brought us off a quantity of turtle-eggs. The female turtle frequent Southwest Bay in large numbers, for the purpose of depositing their eggs in the sand. But up till now, they had failed to catch any of the turtle. The eggs are excellent, and can be used for every purpose for which fowl’s eggs are employed. Here is a receipt for making eggnog which I have tried myself and can recommend:﻿—Two turtle-eggs, a teaspoonful of tinned milk, some water, sugar, and a small glass of rum.

===Chinese===
他们给我们捎来了一批海龟蛋。母海龟成群结队地频频光顾西南湾，为的是把蛋产进沙里。可直到此时，他们连一只海龟也还没能捉到。这蛋滋味极好，鸡蛋派得上什么用场，它便派得上什么用场。这里有一味蛋奶酒的方子，我亲自试过，可以推荐：——海龟蛋两枚，罐装牛奶一茶匙，清水些许，糖适量，再兑一小杯朗姆酒。

===Original===
The shore-party had obtained an abundance of fish; they used to catch them not only with hook and line, but with an extemporized seine net, which they dragged with great success through the pools left by the receding tide. This seine was simply a long piece of the wire-netting which we had brought with us to serve as land-crab-proof fencing round the camp. It seems that this netting did not fulfil its original purpose very satisfactorily, as the crabs could burrow under it.

===Chinese===
岸上的队伍弄到了充裕的鱼；他们平日捕鱼不但用钓钩钓线，还用一张临时凑成的拉网，在退潮留下的大小水潭里拖曳，收获颇丰。这张网其实不过是随身带来的铁丝网的一长条——原是打算围在营地四周防陆蟹的。这铁丝网原先的差事看来干得不大令人满意，因为陆蟹能从网底下打洞钻过去。

===Original===
The land-crabs however, did not molest the shore-party to any extent, and it was only now and then that a man found one of these unpleasant creatures in his bed. It was the custom for the men to sally forth every evening, just before dark, and kill, with sticks, every land-crab they could find in the immediate neighbourhood of the camp, each man slaying his sixty or seventy. This afforded an abundance of food for the others during the night, so that they had no need to stray into the tents. The crabs, I was informed, were excellent scavengers, and consumed all the cook’s refuse.

===Chinese===
不过陆蟹并没有怎么滋扰岸上的队伍，只是偶尔才有人在自家床上发现一只这种讨厌的东西。众人有个惯例：每天傍晚天黑之前倾巢出动，用棍子敲死营地左近所能找到的每一只陆蟹，每人能宰上六七十只。这就为余下的蟹备下了充裕的过夜食粮，它们便无须流窜进帐篷里去了。据他们说，这些蟹是极好的清道夫，厨子的泔水污物全教它们吃了个精光。

===Original===
The doctor and his companions had no lack of news to impart. I was anxious, of course, in the first place, to learn how the work had progressed. I was told that some hundreds of tons of earth had been already removed, and that a broad trench was being dug, along the face of the cliff, through the landslip in the first bend of the ravine, but that, so far, no indications of the treasure had been come across. The chief difficulty consisted in the presence of a great many stones of all sizes that were mixed up with the fallen soil, some of them being of several tons weight. In digging the trench an inclined plane was left at either end, up which the barrows of earth could be wheeled; and when one of the big stones was found, the earth was, in the first place, cleared from round it, and then it was dragged from the bottom of the trench up one of these inclined planes by means of powerful tackle, assisted by the hydraulic jack. When they had got it by these means to the top of the trench, they could easily roll it down the ravine.

===Chinese===
医生和他的同伴们有的是新闻可讲。我自然头一件就急于打听工程进展如何。据说已有几百吨土方被挖走，一道宽阔的壕沟正沿着峭壁的立面、穿过裂谷头一道弯处的滑坡开掘，但至今尚未碰到宝藏的半点迹象。最大的难处，在于坍土里混杂着大大小小数不清的石块，有的重达数吨。挖沟时两头各留一道斜坡，好运送土方的独轮车推上去；碰上大石块，先把四周的土清掉，再用强力的滑轮组、借着液压千斤顶之力，把它从沟底沿斜坡拖上去。用这法子弄到沟顶之后，顺势往裂谷里一滚便了。

===Original===
The doctor explained to me all the routine that he had laid down for observance on shore, and the different details of the work. Sunday was always a holiday, and was occupied, as a rule, in wandering about and exploring; but it was sometimes too terribly hot for this.

===Chinese===
医生向我详述了他为岸上制定的整套章程，以及各项工作的零碎细节。星期日永远是休息日，照例用来四处漫游、探察；可有时热得实在吓人，连这个也做不成。

===Original===
I was informed that a crowbar and several other fresh relics of Mr. A﻿—﻿—’s expedition had been discovered, and that a wooden box had been found, carefully hidden away at the farther end of the bay, which contained a chessboard, a quantity of shot cartridges, and several London and Newcastle newspapers, dated October 1875. Mr. A﻿—﻿—’s expedition took place in 1885, Mr. P﻿—﻿—’s﻿—the first expedition﻿—in 1880; so the papers gave us no clue as to who had brought them here. The shore-party had amused themselves by reading these ancient journals. In them they found accounts of the Wainwright trial and of the collision between the *Mistletoe* and the *Alberta*. It was strange to read, on Trinidad, the old theatrical advertisements in the *Standard*, with Charles Matthews acting at the Gaiety and Miss Marie Wilton at some other house. There was an excellent notice of the latter charming actress in one of these papers.

===Chinese===
我还得知，他们又发现了一根撬棍和A——先生那支远征队留下的另外几件新遗物，还在海湾尽头寻到一只仔细藏好的木箱，里面装着一副棋盘、一批霰弹枪弹，还有几份伦敦和纽卡斯尔的报纸，日期是 1875 年 10 月。A——先生的远征是在 1885 年，P——先生的那次——最早的一次——在 1880 年；所以这些报纸并不能给我们什么线索，说明是谁把它们带到此地来的。岸上的队伍拿这些古旧报章读着消遣，在里面读到了温赖特案的报道，还有「桑寄生号」（Mistletoe）与「艾伯塔号」（Alberta）相撞的消息。在特林达迪读《标准报》上那些旧日的戏剧广告——查尔斯·马修斯在欢乐剧院（Gaiety）登台，玛丽·威尔顿小姐在另一家戏院演出——真是别有一番奇异。其中一份报上，还有一篇对后一位妩媚女演员的精彩剧评。

===Original===
I was told that there had not been so much surf in Southwest Bay as might have been expected with so strong a wind; but, as I have explained, the southeast is the wind that raises the least surf on this sandy beach, though it blows right on to it.

===Chinese===
我听说西南湾里的碎浪，并不像这么大的风本该掀起的那么多；不过正如我先前解释过的，吹在这片沙滩上的诸般风里，数东南风掀起的浪最小，虽然它正对着海岸刮。

===Original===
The doctor told me that they had experienced, on every occasion they had landed, a strong current sweeping along the shore of the bay in an easterly direction, so that, no sooner did the bow of the boat touch the sand, than her stern was driven round by the current to the left, and, unless proper precautions were taken, she would get broadside on to the next sea and be rolled over.

===Chinese===
医生告诉我，他们每回登岸，都遇上一股沿着海湾岸边向东扫去的强劲水流，以致艇头刚一触到沙滩，艇尾就被水流冲得向左转将过去；若不采取适当的防范，她便会横身迎上下一个浪头，被掀得滚翻过去。

===Original===
On being asked whether they had had much rain in the bay, they replied that the showers had been as heavy as those tropical downpours we had experienced in the doldrums. They said that the Sugarloaf presented a magnificent appearance after one of these showers, for then a cascade 700 feet in height would pour down its almost perpendicular sides. They had been enabled to fill their tanks and breakers with rainwater, and had only used the condensing apparatus on one or two occasions, and then more by way of experiment, to see how it worked, than from necessity. It acted perfectly, and with it five gallons of fresh water were distilled from seawater in a very short time.

===Chinese===
被问到湾里雨多不多时，他们回答说，阵雨之大，不亚于我们在赤道无风带经历过的那些热带倾盆。他们说，一阵这样的雨过后，糖面包山的景象蔚为壮观——一道七百英尺高的瀑布，会顺着它几近垂直的山壁倾泻而下。他们借雨水灌满了水箱和淡水桶，蒸馏器只用过一两回，而且多半是当试验、看看它的功效，并非出于必需。那器物运转得尽善尽美，用它从海水里蒸馏淡水，短短工夫便得五加仑。

===Original===
The fortunate discovery had also been made of two small issues of water among the cliffs at the east end of the bay. The supply was sufficient, and though the carrying of the water in breakers from here to the camp over the rough ground entailed heavy labour, it was easier to fetch it in this way than to collect the large quantity of firewood necessary for condensing an equal amount of water.

===Chinese===
还有一桩幸运的发现：湾东尽头的峭壁间，有两股细小的出水。水量是够用的；虽说提着淡水桶、蹚着崎岖地面从这里往营地运水是桩重活，可若要蒸馏出同量的水，得收集一大堆柴火，两下相比，这样取水还是省力些。

===Original===
